Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the analytical space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 37 and the convergence rate is O(503/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on analytical structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 37. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 503. QED.
